- **research.rollback-broken**: Investigate a question against high-trust primary sources and capture the findings as a Markdown file in the repo. Use when the user wants a topic researched, docs or API facts gathered, or reading legwork delegated to a background agent. ([AI-Degen-69/agents-home](https://github.com/AI-Degen-69/agents-home/tree/HEAD/skills/research) · ★1)
- **research.texreview**: Open texreview, a local web UI to review the compiled paper - LaTeX source left, PDF right, SyncTeX click-to-source, comments on selections in either pane (button, `⌘⇧M`, or right-click) (requires uv + a TeX install). Comments are sidecar JSON in the paper repo's .texreview/ that any agent can read. ([jiancui-research/research-kit](https://github.com/jiancui-research/research-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/research.texreview) · ★4)
- **research.write**: Write or revise a manuscript section in the paper's style; also learn and maintain writing preferences from samples, instructions, and edits. ([jiancui-research/research-kit](https://github.com/jiancui-research/research-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/research.write) · ★4)
- **research/citation-fabrication-guard**: 参考文献真实性闸门：AI 给出的任何文献先按「未核实草稿」处理，四核对全过才允许写进正文或据此下结论。 触发：AI 给的文献/参考文献 / 假文献 / 编造引用 / 文献核实 / DOI 核对 / 综述引用可疑 / 为什么 AI 总给假文献。 （来源：视频号「学姐keqidian」2026-11-02 主题「为什么 DeepSeek 总给假文献」；方法学自建，不采用视频口播的具体结论。） ([luotiandong799-coder/AI-Skills](https://github.com/luotiandong799-coder/AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/research/citation-fabrication-guard) · ★1)
- **research/ki-knowledge-graph**: 知识图谱 / GraphRAG（Knowledge Intelligence）。当需要从大量文档/语料构建知识图谱、做跨文档多跳问答、全局主题归纳、实体关系推理，或问"GraphRAG 怎么用 / 知识图谱怎么搭 / 文档间关系"时调用。提供 GraphRAG 四阶段（实体关系抽取→Leiden 社区检测→分层社区摘要→local/global 查询）、LightRAG 双层检索与增量更新、图谱何时优于向量 RAG 的判据，以及实体漂移/陈旧图/成本等坑。不用于单点事实查询（向量 RAG 更优）。 ([luotiandong799-coder/AI-Skills](https://github.com/luotiandong799-coder/AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/research/ki-knowledge-graph) · ★1)
- **research2paper**: Turn real research material (method notes, experiment records, result tables, figures, references, an existing draft or reviewer comments) into an evidence-bound SCI original research paper draft, or work on one part of it — Introduction, Methods, Results and Discussion, Conclusion/Abstract/Title, figure and table captions — draft point-by-point responses to reviewers, and run a pre-submission check. Follows DR_CAN's SCI writing lessons; English manuscript text with Chinese notes by default; every missing fact becomes a MISSING marker instead of a guess. Use whenever the user wants to 写论文/写SC… ([7toCR/2pi](https://github.com/7toCR/2pi/tree/HEAD/skills/research2paper))
- **Research2Podcast**: 1. **Discovery (Exa)**: - MUST use the `exa-web-search` skill (or the relevant Exa script) to perform a semantic search on the provided topic. Do not use native web search for this step. - **Freshness Filter**: Apply a dynamic `publishedAfter` filter calculated as **Current Date minus 90 days**. - **Quality Screening**: Select top 5-10 "high signal" sources (official docs, expert blogs, technical ([Landsvig1/agent-skills](https://github.com/Landsvig1/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Research2Podcast))

([masseater/codex-plugin](https://github.com/masseater/codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/research/skills/incremental-research))
- **research:ingest**: Ingest a paper into this research wiki. Slug validation → source provisioning → triage → the deep / shallow / reject decision → summary draft (deep and shallow) → concept reconciliation (deep only) → index update → T1 lint → log → archive raw source. Every artifact is staged as `.draft.md` and waits for explicit approval. Trigger whenever the user says "ingest", "add this paper", "file this PDF", points at a markdown file in `raw/` that's a paper, drops an arxiv link with intent to add it, or asks to bring a paper into the wiki — even without using the word "ingest". ([LongarMD/research-wiki](https://github.com/LongarMD/research-wiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-ingest))
- **research:library-research**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "research this library", "ライブラリを徹底調査", "framework investigation", "knowledge base", or wants clone-first hands-on research persisted as reusable knowledge. ([masseater/codex-plugin](https://github.com/masseater/codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/research/skills/library-research))
- **research:lint**: Health-check the research wiki. T1 = five fast scripted checks via wiki-meta/bin/lint-t1.sh (orphan pages, dead wikilinks, stale drafts older than 7 days, index sync, log monotonicity). T2 = on-demand LLM pass for deeper issues (concept gaps, missing cross-refs, plans without kill-conditions, contradictions across summaries). Use whenever the user says "lint", "run lint", "lint t2", "check the wiki", "find orphans", "check index sync", "check for drift", or asks about wiki health. T2 is triggered by "lint t2" and can be scoped to a topic directory, e.g. "lint t2 your-topic". ([LongarMD/research-wiki](https://github.com/LongarMD/research-wiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-lint))
- **research:pdf-convert**: Turn a PDF link (usually an academic white paper or arXiv article) into clean, ingest-ready markdown. Runs `uv run convert &lt;link&gt;` to produce markdown under `raw/`, applies `uv run clean` plus agent-judgment cleanup to strip conversion artifacts, then `uv run split` to break the result into main / references / appendix files by line number. Trigger whenever the user gives a PDF or arXiv link and wants it converted, cleaned, or prepped for ingest — even without saying "convert". This is the pre-step that feeds `research:ingest`. ([LongarMD/research-wiki](https://github.com/LongarMD/research-wiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-pdf-convert))
- **research:prior-art**: Research existing solutions when exploring a new problem space. Use when the user mentions "prior art", "existing solutions", "what libraries exist for", or wants to understand the landscape before building. ([bendrucker/claude](https://github.com/bendrucker/claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/research/skills/prior-art) · ★18)
- **research:query**: Answer research questions from this wiki. Reads index.md first, drills into candidate pages, synthesizes with wikilink citations, separates grounded findings from speculation, and optionally proposes filing the answer back as a new or updated page via the draft-approval flow. Trigger on natural-language research questions about papers, concepts, methods, comparisons, or topics covered by this wiki — even when the user phrases it as a passing question rather than an explicit "query" command. ([LongarMD/research-wiki](https://github.com/LongarMD/research-wiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-query))
- **research:research-loop**: Answers a question from what web sources say, with every claim cited. Use when the user runs /research:research-loop with a question, or asks to research something on the web, look something up online, find current or up-to-date information, or check what public sources say about a topic. Run the research in a subagent and tell that subagent to load the `research:research-loop` skill. ([fprochazka/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/fprochazka/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/research/skills/research-loop) · ★14)
- **research:semtools**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "semantic search", "自然言語で検索", "semtools", "search files by meaning", or wants to search file contents using natural-language queries. ([masseater/codex-plugin](https://github.com/masseater/codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/research/skills/semtools))

- **researchzy**: Researchzy, the entry point for research with real papers. Searches about 250 million papers by DOI, arXiv ID, title or keywords, manages the user's library, gets and adds PDFs, and routes every other research task (literature reviews, systematic reviews and meta-analyses, ideas, citations, journal choice, reading, writing, translation and polishing, figures, statistics and causal inference, slides, grants, submission and revision) to the Researchzy skill that does it. Use when the user mentions Researchzy, wants to find, save or get papers, or asks for research help without naming a skill. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy/skills/researchzy))
- **researchzy-causal-inference**: Estimate causal effects from panel or cross-section data: probe the design, fix the estimand first, run DiD (staggered, event study), IV, RD, panel FE, synthetic control or weighting with diagnostics, log every specification, check the text. Use for policy evaluations and natural experiments. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-causal-inference))
- **researchzy-citation-finder**: Find citations for manuscript sentences that need one ([Citation needed: …] placeholders, uncited claims): search Researchzy's index, read each candidate against the sentence, keep a checked quote, write BibTeX. Use to fill the citation gaps of a draft. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy/skills/researchzy-citation-finder))
- **researchzy-data-availability**: Draft or audit manuscript Data/Code Availability statements, dataset access routes, repository plans, and FAIR metadata. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-manuscript/skills/researchzy-data-availability))
- **researchzy-deep-read**: Close reading of one paper as a Markdown long-read: how the authors actually got there, which design choices carry the result, the key formulas worked through with concrete numbers, and how far the evidence reaches, including what the paper does not report. Use to understand a paper before building on, presenting, reviewing or citing it. Not a summary, a translation or a peer review. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-reading/skills/researchzy-deep-read))

Use when the user wants papers' PDFs or full text, or another skill needs a paper Researchzy has no PDF for. ([Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills](https://github.com/Researchzy/Researchzy-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchzy-reading/skills/researchzy-download))

- **researchzy-find-journal**: Shortlist journals or conferences for a manuscript from evidence: where similar recent papers and its references appeared, recent on-topic papers per venue, Crossref and DOAJ records. - **researchzy-latex**: Build a LaTeX paper with its own engine and bibliography tool, turn the log into a short problem list, fix only mechanical problems with a content check, move a paper into a venue kit the user supplies, and pack a source archive that rebuilds.
